| 2026-05-26 | **Cross-subdomain localStorage isolation: Dashboard JWT yan-origin'den okunmaz (CQ8/CQ9 architectural FAIL)** — FEAT-VFB F0.5 Chunk D iter 0'da AD-33 kararı "localStorage.access_token Dashboard JWT" idi. SAME token fetch (Authorization Bearer) + WS handshake (?token=) için kullanılır — single source. localStorage same-origin'de kullanılmaya devam (AHA-4 voice-poc-tenant-id/flow-id persistence, voice.invekto.com:8443'in kendi localStorage'ı). | **3 meta-lesson:** (1) **Cross-origin auth pattern**: subdomain'ler arasında JWT/session paylaşımı için 3 portable kanal var — (a) URL bridge `?token=`, (b) `SameSite=None; Secure` cookie + parent origin Set-Cookie, (c) postMessage + parent iframe. localStorage / sessionStorage / IndexedDB hepsi origin-scoped, **port farkı bile yeter** (`:443` ve `:8443` farklı origin). (2) **Browser security boundary review checklist**: yeni cross-subdomain feature için Codex submit ÖNCE — "Hangi storage cross-origin? Cookie domain `.invekto.com` mı?" sorularını AskUserQuestion ile çözmeden code yazma. Q karar verirken bile reviewer architectural sanity check yapmalı (defensive). (3) **Dashboard wrapper pattern** Invekto'da kanonik: app.invekto.com SPA "Voice Test" link click → window.open(`https://voice.invekto.com:8443/voice-poc.html?token=${localStorage.access_token}`) → page-side bridge script alır + URL'i temizler. Aynı pattern: Hangfire `/ops/hangfire-login` backend cookie bridge (farklı pattern: backend redirect Set-Cookie); INMA iframe `?cibToken` postMessage. |
| 2026-05-26 | **`Number.parseInt` prefix-based parsing bug: "12abc" → 12, "1e3" → 1, "0x10" → 0 (VQ-DATA FAIL)** — FEAT-VFB F0.5 Chunk D iter 0'da `parsePositiveInt` localStorage tenant_id/flow_id restore için `Number.parseInt(String(raw), 10)` kullandı. Codex VQ-DATA-D2 FAIL: "parsePositiveInt accepts partially corrupted strings because Number.parseInt is prefix-based. A stale/corrupted value like `\"5abc\"` can become tenant_id 5 if that tenant exists." JavaScript spec: parseInt leading-prefix integer çekmeye çalışır, geri kalanı atar. `Number.isInteger(parseInt("12abc",10)) === true` (returns 12, integer). | **Iter 1 strict-regex canonical pattern:** ```js function parsePositiveInt(raw) { if (raw === null || raw === undefined || raw === '') return null; const s = String(raw); if (!/^[1-9][0-9]*$/.test(s)) return null; const n = Number(s); return Number.isSafeInteger(n) && n > 0 ? n : null; }``` Regex: ^[1-9][0-9]*$ — leading-zero yok, whitespace yok, decimal/exponent yok, sign yok. Number(s) strict mode'da NaN döner non-numeric için (parseInt'in aksine). Number.isSafeInteger 2^53-1 cap. | **2 meta-lesson:** (1) **JavaScript integer parsing tehlikeli alanı**: `Number.parseInt` legacy (ECMAScript 1), `Number()` strict, `Number.parseFloat` decimal accept. Untrusted input (localStorage / URL param / form / JSON-from-untrusted-server) için HER ZAMAN strict regex + Number(s) + isSafeInteger. parseInt SADECE pre-validated input'larda. (2) **Form/storage integer validation checklist**: `<input type="number">` browser UI hint, JS validation YOKsa "12abc" geçer (form değeri string, type number sadece input UI). LocalStorage'da serialize edilen integer'lar 6 ay sonra corrupt halinde geri okunabilir (browser bug, extension manipulation, cross-tab race). Strict regex parse defansif baseline. |

| 2026-05-17 | **AI-generated UI content trust boundary: backend prompt KESIN OLARAK GUVENILMEZ â€” FE display layer her zaman defansif sanitizer'a sahip olmali** â€” AI panel paketinde Q'nun feedback'i geldi: "ham flowconfig JSON'u gosterdi, kullanici dis hekimi, uygulamayi terk eder, para kaybettirir." Backend prompt "JSON yazma" kurali vardi ama AI ihlal etti (code fence yok, satir satir ham JSON dump). Root cause: backend prompt soft guarantee, FE'de hic safety net yoktu. Tek katmanli defense (sadece prompt) yetersiz. | 2-katmanli defense: (1) FE display layer **mutlaka** kendi sanitizer'ina sahip olmali (stripRawJsonBlocks line-start `{`/`[` + balanced bracket count + same-line balanced quote-check; stripJsonKeywordSequences 2+ ardisik `"key":`); (2) Backend prompt yine var (talimat + ornek karsi-ornek). FE hem display layer (cleanAssistantText) hem streaming layer (stripStreamingBlocks) ayni sanitizer'i kullanir â€” tek source of truth, kullanici JSON'u **anlik bile gormez**. | **Canonical pattern (2026-05-17):** AI-generated content (LLM output) FE'ye geldiginde **her zaman**: (a) Code-fence stripping (`` ```code``` `` blocks), (b) Raw-structure stripping (JSON object/array, XML tags, etc.), (c) Multi-line + single-line variants ikisi de korunmali. Streaming sirasinda da uygulanmali (anlik flash engellemek icin). Backend prompt sadece "best effort"; FE'de zero-tolerance sanitizer **zorunlu**. Heuristic kombinasyonu: (1) bracket-based balanced count, (2) keyword-pattern density, (3) quote-presence in bracketed content. Markdown link `[text](url)` quote yok -> keep; raw JSON `{"k":1}` quote var -> strip. |

Iter 4'te geri alindi. | Backend her iki turn'u (user TEMPLATE_SEED + assistant greeting) persist eder â€” Claude API context intact. FE display layer ise TEMPLATE_SEED user mesajini visible messages[]'a koymaz (load filter `.filter(m => !(m.role === 'user' && m.content.startsWith('TEMPLATE_SEED:')))`). Bu sayede: (a) Browser refresh sonrasi backend wizard_history'den TEMPLATE_SEED user mesaji geri okunur, FE template adini extract edip templateContext'i restore eder (banner survives refresh), (b) Sonraki Claude API call'larinda backend tam history gonderir (conversation natural devam), (c) Kullanici asla TEMPLATE_SEED balonu gormez. | **Canonical pattern (2026-05-17):** Hidden seed/system mesajlari hicbir zaman backend persist'inden silinmemeli (LLM conversation invariant). Display filter SADECE frontend display layer'da uygulanmali. Persist data ayni zamanda banner/context restore icin bonus source-of-truth gorevi yapabilir (no extra DB column needed). | 2026-04-24 | **Paket B-META curl.exe `--data` body modification vs `--data-binary @file` for HMAC smoke**. AC1(c) webhook smoke test first attempt: PowerShell `curl.exe -X POST ... --data '$body' "..."` â†’ HTTP 401 INV-META-001 signature mismatch, even though my PowerShell-computed HMAC-SHA256 over the exact same string matched what the server should have computed. Root cause: `curl.exe --data` does NOT send raw body byte-exact â€” it strips/normalizes whitespace (CR/LF stripping, multi-space collapse, backslash escape interpretation in some shells). HMAC-SHA256 is byte-sensitive; server received a different body than what my local HMAC computed over. Result: correct crypto, wrong input â†’ mismatch. Debugging took ~15 min. | Fix: Write payload to file via `[System.IO.File]::WriteAllText($tmpFile, $payload, (New-Object System.Text.UTF8Encoding($false)))` (UTF-8 no BOM) + curl.exe `--data-binary "@$tmpFile"` â€” byte-exact preservation. Verify via `[System.IO.File]::ReadAllBytes($tmpFile).Length` matches `$payload.Length`. Second attempt PASS: HTTP 200 `{"data":{"accepted":true}}` + audit row INSERT verified. | **Webhook HMAC smoke canonical idiom for PowerShell**: (1) write body to temp file with UTF-8-no-BOM encoding, (2) read bytes + compute HMAC-SHA256 using those exact bytes, (3) curl.exe `--data-binary "@file"` (the `@` prefix tells curl "read from file"), (4) verify byte length pre-send. **NEVER** use `--data`, `-d`, `--data-raw`, or `--data-urlencode` for HMAC-signed requests â€” each has different whitespace/escape handling. `--data-binary` is the ONLY curl mode guaranteed byte-exact. For Python: `requests.post(url, data=body.encode('utf-8'))` (NOT `json=body` which re-serializes). For Node: `axios.post(url, body, {headers, transformRequest: [(d) => d]})`. **Cross-language moral**: signed-body protocols require byte-exact body transport; HTTP client abstractions that "helpfully" normalize whitespace/encoding silently break signatures. Always test locally with a round-trip: compute HMAC over sent body via a server-side echo endpoint, compare with client-computed. If they differ, client is mangling body. |

Pattern: **yeni seed SQL yazarken default olarak column-inference form kullan**; named-constraint form sadece schema'da explicit `ADD CONSTRAINT` varsa gÃ¼venli. **Schema verify recipe:** `\d+ <table>` ile "Indexes" + "Check constraints" bÃ¶lÃ¼mlerini gÃ¶r; sadece "Indexes" altÄ±nda listelenen `uq_*` entry'leri INDEX, `ADD CONSTRAINT` output'ta farklÄ± gÃ¶rÃ¼nÃ¼r. `arch/db/*.sql` dosyasÄ±nda `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX` gÃ¶rdÃ¼ÄŸÃ¼nde **asla** `ON CONFLICT ON CONSTRAINT <same name>` kullanma â€” column-inference veya `ALTER TABLE ... ADD CONSTRAINT` Ã¶nceki migration yaz. Codex bu pattern'i yakalayamayabilir (schema text'teki isim yeterli ipucu deÄŸil); pre-prod dry-run (dev DB veya test schema) bu bug'Ä± yakalar. **Latent 6 ay risk:** Bu pattern baÅŸka `uq_*` INDEX'lerle de tekrarlanabilir â€” `grep -n "ON CONFLICT ON CONSTRAINT uq_" arch/db/ src/` ile tÃ¼m occurrence'lar audit edilmeli. |

| 2026-03-26 | Kestrel IP whitelist'te IPv6-mapped IPv4 uyuÅŸmazlÄ±ÄŸÄ±: Config'de `91.151.84.79` ama Kestrel `::ffff:91.151.84.79` olarak gÃ¶rÃ¼yor â†’ HashSet.Contains eÅŸleÅŸmiyor â†’ 401 | IP whitelist yÃ¼klenirken her IP iÃ§in hem raw hem `::ffff:` mapped versiyonunu HashSet'e ekle. `IPAddress.IsIPv4MappedToIPv6` + `MapToIPv4()` ile normalize | **IP whitelist/allowlist kullanan HER yerde (webhook, CORS, rate-limit) IPv4 ve IPv6-mapped versiyonunu birlikte kaydet. Kestrel dual-stack socket IPv4 baÄŸlantÄ±larÄ±nÄ± `::ffff:x.x.x.x` formatÄ±nda raporlar** |

| 2026-06-10 | Veri-import: Excel'de SAYI-formatlı telefon, SheetJS `raw:false` ile Excel'in GÖRÜNTÜ metnini döndürdü (`9.05332E+11`) → önizleme bozuk + sunucu digit-strip `90533211` (8 hane) üretti, Guard 8..15'ten GEÇTİ → sessiz-bozuk-sendable numara riski | İki katman: (1) parse-anı `cell.w` repair — ham `cell.v`'den tam sayı (SADECE xlsx/xls + pozitif tamsayı + onarım `^\d{8,15}$`); CSV'de ASLA (SheetJS CSV'deki scientific METNİ de sayı hücresine çevirir ama `v` kayıplı — repair sahte numara uydurur, repro kanıtlı); (2) PhoneNormalizer + client preview'a scientific reject (digit-strip ÖNCESİ) + operatöre amber ipucu | **SheetJS `raw:false` = Excel görüntü metni, ham değer DEĞİL — sayısal telefon/ID kolonları 11+ hanede scientific'e düşer. xlsx'te ham `cell.v`'den kurtar; CSV'de kurtarma YOK (reject + 'kolonu Metin yap' ipucu). Lenient digit-strip normalizer'a bilinen-artifact reject'ini strip ÖNCESİ koy — garbage, plausible-valid'e dönüşmesin** |

## Patterns That Work

### Architecture & Design

| Pattern | Where Used | Why It Works |
|---------|------------|--------------|
| Service isolation | Mikro servisler | Bagimsiz deploy, kolay test |
| Shared contracts | Servisler arasi | Type safety, API uyumu |
| IDisposable pattern | Resource management | Memory leak prevention |
| Denormalized counters | Aggregate tables | O(1) reads, atomic increment |
| Generic key-value settings API | Ayarlar | Yeni ayar = backend degisikligi gerekmez |
| Immutable graph pre-compute (HashSet/Dict) | FlowGraphV2 | O(1) lookup, thread-safe reuse |
| Contextual required field validation | FlowValidator | Runtime context check, false positive onler |
| Roadmap/teknik-detay hiyerarsisi | Docs | Summary -> tracking -> detail, tek kaynak |
| Plan review + iyilestirme adimi | Phase 3a | 8 mimari iyilestirme bulundu, plansiz baslamak kacirirdi |
| Keyword pre-filter before LLM | BatchClassification RI-8.1 | High-confidence regex matches skip expensive LLM call â†’ cost savings, same accuracy for clear-cut outcomes |
| Parallel.ForEachAsync with bounded concurrency | BatchClassification RI-8.4 | maxDegreeOfParallelism=4 prevents overwhelming MSSQL while 4x faster loading |
| 10 Paket Execution Stratejisi (v5.1) | Phase 2-3 | 24 dongu -> 8 paket -> buyuk PKT split ile 10 paket, yonetilebilir boyut |
| Pre-phase tech research + faz dosyasina embed | Phase 3C/3D CLIP/MediaPipe | Faz basladiginda karar alinmis, zaman kaybi yok |
| PKT split: isimli strateji + Q secimi | PKT-6 -> 6A/6B/6C (Strategy C) | Coklu secenek -> Q bilincli karar verir, tek oneri dayatma yok |
| Karsilastirma tablosu (latency/cost/CPU/prod-ready) | CLIP + MediaPipe arastirmasi | Q hizli karar verir, duz metin yerine tablo net |
| GT auto-accept (tieredâ†’GT) for rapid benchmarking | RI-Faz2 cross-validation | Tiered F1=1.0 tautological ama bagimsiz modelleri hizlica karsilastirabiliyorsun. Human GT = gelecekte |

### DB & Data

| Pattern | Where Used | Why It Works |
|---------|------------|--------------|
| IDbContextFactory + await using | Concurrent requests | Thread-safe DbContext |
| AddPooledDbContextFactory + scoped | DI setup | Factory + legacy scoped birlikte calisir |
| ConcurrentDictionary.TryAdd | Race prevention | Ilk gelen kazanir |
| Idempotent DB op (catch dup -> fetch) | Concurrent writes | Duplicate key yerine mevcut kaydi dondur |
| ON CONFLICT reactivation | FAQ CRUD | Soft-delete + unique birlikte calisir |
| Multi-value batch INSERT (50/batch) | KnowledgeRepository | N/50 round-trip, atomik |
| Multi-row UPDATE FROM VALUES | BatchUpdateChunkEmbeddings | Tek SQL, N/50 round-trip |
| Document tenant ownership check | Chunk insert | Multi-tenant izolasyon garantisi |
| SQL CASE WHEN conditional fetch | AutomationRepository | Gereksiz buyuk JSON cekilmez |
| Soft-delete + ON CONFLICT pattern | Soft-delete tablolar | Unique constraint + reactivation |
| Semantic search + keyword fallback | RetrievalService | Embedding fail -> keyword'e dus, graceful |
| `template.Lang ?? lang` effective value after fallback | TriggerProcessor lang fallback | Orijinal deger yerine "secilen" degeri kullan - data tutarliligi |
| 3-step fallback chain (specific â†’ default â†’ any) | Outbound lang template | Graceful degradation: her adimda daha genel, ama warning ile |
| Warning field in API response for fallback | TriggerWebhookResponse.Warning | Caller fallback oldugunu bilir, loglama yetmez |

### Deploy & Config

| Pattern | Where Used | Why It Works |
|---------|------------|--------------|
| ConfigureKestrel + ListenAnyIP | Port binding | Explicit port, launchSettings bagimsiz |
| curl.exe -k PowerShell'de | HTTPS API call | TLS sorunlari bypass |
| Ayri .ps1 + .bat wrapper | Deploy scripts | Pipe/escape sorunlari ortadan kalkar |
| appsettings.Production.json | Deploy-safe config | Sunucu secrets korunur |
| Yeni Mikroservis Checklist | Her yeni servis | 17 maddelik otomatik checklist |
| Yeni Servis Deploy Yardimi | Her yeni servis deploy'u | SQL fix + appsettings + NSSM + firewall + restart OTOMATIK sunulur (watcher artÄ±k gereksiz â€” SSH/MCP deploy) |
| Selective git staging | /rev workflow | Focused diff, Codex review kolaylasir |
| Port haritasi cross-check | Yeni servis planlama | Port cakismasi onlenir |

### Auth & Security

| Pattern | Where Used | Why It Works |
|---------|------------|--------------|
| JwtGenerator shared token factory | Invekto.Shared | JWT signing tek yerde |
| Auth guard null-safe (exists && mismatch) | Tenant isolation | null = yok, 403 sadece gercek mismatch |
| File cleanup on upload failure | Knowledge upload | Orphan dosya birakma |
| LLM anti-leak prompt kuralÄ± | TÃ¼m mÃ¼ÅŸteriye gÃ¶rÃ¼nen LLM servisleri | "Do NOT include reasoning, thinking, meta-commentary" â€” chain-of-thought sÄ±zÄ±ntÄ±sÄ±nÄ± Ã¶nler. Haiku Ã¶zellikle sÄ±zÄ±ntÄ±ya yatkÄ±n |
| LLM Ã§eviri dilbilgisi kuralÄ± | TranslationService system prompt | TÃ¼rkÃ§e SOV cÃ¼mle yapÄ±sÄ± + "lÃ¼tfen" baÅŸa â€” LLM'ler Ä°ngilizce SVO sÄ±rasÄ±yla devrik Ã§eviri yapÄ±yor |
| LLM Ã§Ä±ktÄ± test ederken cache temizle | Translation cache (message_translations) | AynÄ± metin cache'den gelir, yeni prompt test edilemez â€” DELETE FROM message_translations |
| Impersonate via existing JWT infra | SuperAdmin tenant switch | Yeni middleware gereksiz â€” GenerateToken + setSession + removeTokens mevcut altyapiyi yeniden kullaniyor |
| Basic Auth in-memory + JWT localStorage | Impersonate exit flow | removeTokens JWT siler ama credentials (in-memory) kalir â€” ops mode'a donus icin login gereksiz |
| window.location.href (navigate degil) | Impersonate giris/cikis | Full page reload tum hook'lari yeni session ile baslatir â€” React Router navigate stale state birakir |
| INMA JWT CompanyCode = tenant_id mapping | Token exchange + Dashboard session | CompanyId = INMA internal, CompanyCode = bizim tenant_id â€” webhook `?companyId=` parametresi de CompanyCode'a karsilik gelir |

### Workflow & Review

| Pattern | Where Used | Why It Works |
|---------|------------|--------------|
| Interview'da seytanin avukatligi | Auto workflow | Q challenge edilir, pasif kalmak degil |
| Codex escalation analizi | Iter 3 escalation | real vs false-positive kategorize et |
| 3-dosya arch update pattern | Her faz bitisi | session-memory + active-work + q-ops-checklist |
| OPS-N numarali ops checklist | q-ops-checklist.md | Kisa referans, cross-session takip |
| queueMicrotask deferred revalidation | flow-store.ts | UI donmuyor, validation otomatik |
| Step result chaining | Simulator | Onceki step'ten otomatik deger cekme |
| Error callback in async processing | Orchestrator | Sessiz timeout yerine gercek hata mesaji |
| Pre-write 5 soru gating (Codex Utansin) | Her kod satiri oncesi | Hata yazildiktan sonra degil, yazilmadan ONCE engellenir - iteration=0 |
| DRY canonical source + referans pattern | INVEKTO_BASE -> tum dosyalar | Kural tek yerde tanimlanir, tutarsizlik imkansiz |
| 4 nokta kural yayilimi | Doktrin yazimi | Yeni kural -> INVEKTO_BASE + CLAUDE.md + DEV_AGENT + MEMORY.md = hicbir session kacirmaz |
| Insights raporu -> CLAUDE.md feedback dongusu | Workflow v5.2 | Insights friction analizi -> mevcut kurallara karsilastir -> eksik kurallari ekle, var olanlari skip et |
| `/wrap` skill ile post-phase konsolidasyonu | Session kapama | 5 adim (tracking + learn + secret scan + push + prompt) tek komut, Q manual adim hatirlamaz |
| .gitignore proaktif audit | Git hygiene | deploy_output + usage-data + diffs eklenmezse secret leak + 10K+ pending dosya riski |
| Split Codex review (servis bazli) | PKT-5A 345KB diff | Full diff context asar, servis bazli split = her part <200KB, anlamli review |
| NpgsqlBatch bulk insert | Audit trail, batch ops | N+1 insert dongusu yerine tek batch = tek roundtrip, atomik |
| Cross-service client (Serviceâ†’Service HTTP) | KnowledgeIntentClient (Automationâ†’Knowledge) | Servisler arasi veri cekme icin typed HTTP client + fallback + timeout, DB dogrudan erisim YASAK |
| DB-driven intent pattern (seed + runtime) | PKT-6A AiIntentHandler | Hardcoded intent yerine DB'den cek, sektor bazli seed data ile bootstrap, runtime'da CRUD |
| WapCRM callback bridge (thin proxy) | Backend /api/v1/callback/wapcrm | Automation OutgoingCallback â†’ WapCRM chatoperation format donusumu, instanceID message_log'dan, userID tenant_registry settings'den |
| Dynamic instanceID from message_log | WapCRM bridge | Gelen mesajin instance_id'si message_log'a yazilir, callback'te ayni phone+tenant icin son instance_id okunur â€” hangi hattan geldiyse oradan doner |
| Dual-source pipeline (CSV + MSSQL) via IAsyncEnumerable | CleanerService RunCoreAsync | Ayni temizleme/dedup/insert mantigi `IAsyncEnumerable<List<string[]>>` uzerinden calisiyor â€” CsvStreamReader veya MssqlReaderService farketmez, core logic tek yerde |
| Streaming SqlDataReader forward-only + SequentialAccess | MssqlReaderService | Milyonlarca satir icin buffered collection OLUSTURMA â€” `yield return` + `SqlDataReader` = constant memory, backpressure pipeline tarafindan yonetilir |
| Per-DB ConnectionPool (MCP server) | customer-mssql MCP | `sql.connect()` global tek pool = DB degisince sorun. Her DB icin ayri `new sql.ConnectionPool(config)` + idle timeout ile otomatik temizlik |
| Linked CTS timeout = request already sent | MainAppCallbackClient callback retry | CancellationTokenSource.CreateLinkedTokenSource + CancelAfter(timeout) OperationCanceledException firlatir AMA HTTP request zaten gonderilmistir â€” retry = duplicate mesaj. Timeout catch'inde `return true` (delivered say) |
| New session __last_input initialization | AutomationOrchestrator + AiIntentHandler | Yeni session'da `__last_input` set edilmezse flow auto-chain (triggerâ†’welcomeâ†’ai_intent) kullanicinin ilk mesajini kaybeder. Orchestrator'da `state.Variables["__last_input"] = messageText` + Handler'da first-visit check |

| Pattern | Where Used | Why It Works |
|---------|------------|--------------|
| Popup header'da entity adi | UI Modal | Kullanici neye baktigini hemen anlar |
| {*path:nonfile} SPA fallback | MapFallbackToFile | Static dosyalar fallback'e dusmez |
| Mevcut proxy pattern yeniden kullanma | FbProxyGet | Yeni endpoint 3 satir ile tamamlandi |
| Impersonation banner fixed + pt-10 | Layout.tsx | Fixed banner sidebar sticky'yi bozmaz, pt-10 icerik kaymasi onler |
| opsOnly filter tenant_id=0 bypass | Layout.tsx sidebar | SuperAdmin (tenant_id=0) opsOnly sayfalari gorebilir, impersonate (tenant_idâ‰ 0) goremez |
| Token + response null check | API calls | Auth yoksa sessiz cik, crash olmaz |
| Task agent ile paralel mass-replace | Stripe palette migration (40+ dosya) | Ana context korunur, dosya okuma/edit paralel isler, context bloat onlenir |
| Final grep dogrulama | Mass CSS class replacement | replace_all sonrasi kalan referanslari yakalar â€” ozellikle inline hex (#94a3b8) vs Tailwind class (slate-300) ayri pattern gerektirir |
| Style guide + kod tandem guncelleme | INSE-STYLE-GUIDE.md + Dashboard/FlowBuilder | Kod degistirip guide guncellenmezse drift olusur â€” her palette degisikliginde IKISI BIRDEN guncelle |

### Concurrency & Performance

| Pattern | Where Used | Why It Works |
|---------|------------|--------------|
| Off-peak scheduler | Heavy islemler | Gunduz timeout olmaz |
| Per-operation timeout + continue-on-error | Batch islemler | Bir kayit fail = digerlerine devam |
| Timer-based orphan cleanup | Resource management | Session sizmasini onler |
| IHostedService for cleanup timers | Background services | Clean shutdown |
| SET DEADLOCK_PRIORITY LOW + backoff + jitter | Non-critical jobs | Deadlock'ta victim olur |
| Deadlock retry with error code check | SQL retry | Sadece deadlock'a retry |

### UI/UX & Frontend

| Pattern | Where Used | Why It Works |
|---------|------------|--------------|
| Category-grouped layout with accordion | TemplateLibraryPage | 60 kart okunmaz â€” kategori gruplama + expand/collapse bilgiyi sindirilebilir yapar |
| Turkish labels + descriptions + examples mapping | INTENT_TR, FAQ_TOPICS | Snake_case slug'lar kullaniciya anlamsiz â€” Turkce label + aciklama + ornek musteri mesaji anlasilirlikta buyuk fark yapar |
| Iterative UI feedback loop (Q feedback â†’ immediate fix â†’ deploy) | Onboarding + Templates | Tek seferde mukemmel UI cikmaz. Q'nun 4-5 iterasyon feedback vermesi ve her birinin hemen uygulanmasi en iyi sonuc verir |
| DB data quality cleanup alongside UI redesign | FAQ templates | UI duzeltmek yetmez â€” 42 FAQ'nun 33'u duplicate/garbage idi. UI + data birlikte temizlenmeli |
| Per-category accent colors via inline style + hex | CategorySection | Tailwind class yerine inline hex â€” runtime'da dinamik renk, CSS class patlamasi yok |

## Anti-Patterns to Avoid

### Architecture

| Anti-Pattern | Problem | Better Approach |
|--------------|---------|-----------------|
| Direct DB access between services | Tight coupling | API uzerinden iletisim |
| Shared mutable state | Race conditions | Event-driven communication |
| Hardcoded ports | Cakisma riski | Config/environment'tan oku |
| Lessons-learned okumadan kod | Ayni hata tekrar | Session basinda OKU |

### Code Quality

| Anti-Pattern | Problem | Better Approach |
|--------------|---------|-----------------|
| Raw SQL concat | Injection riski | Parameterized queries |
| Generic error messages | User frustration | Specific + actionable errors |
| Empty catch block | Sessiz failure | Her zaman log veya rethrow |
| Singleton DbContext inject | "second operation" hatasi | IDbContextFactory + await using |
| AddDbContext for concurrent | Factory register edilmiyor | AddPooledDbContextFactory |
| SQL destructure kolon SELECT edilmemis | Undefined, sessiz bug | Destructure = SQL karsiligi kontrol |
| MapFallbackToFile {**slug} | Static dosyalar yakalanir | {*path:nonfile} constraint |

### Operations

| Anti-Pattern | Problem | Better Approach |
|--------------|---------|-----------------|
| Retry without backoff | CPU spike | Exponential backoff |
| Retry without limit | Sonsuz dongu | max_retry_count |
| Queue without drain | Stalled process | timeout + drain_on_stop |
| Startup'ta heavy DB islemi | 10+ dk startup | Scheduler (gece 02:00-05:00) |
| Polling catch'inde sadece log | Sonsuz polling | clearInterval + state reset |

### Process

| Anti-Pattern | Problem | Better Approach |
|--------------|---------|-----------------|
| AskUserQuestion ile strateji tartismasi | Q analiz istiyor, multi-choice degil | Analiz/tablo/karsilastirma sun |
| Kural birden fazla yerde tam tanimlamak (DRY ihlali) | 15+ dosyada tutarsizlik, guncelleme kaosu | Tek canonical source + diger dosyalar referans verir |

---

## Maintenance Rules

1. `/learn` komutu veya auto mode ile guncellenir
2. **3 Ay Kurali:** 50+ giris olunca 3 aydan eski girdiler `arch/lessons-learned-archive.md`'ye tasinir
3. Sadece proje-spesifik ogrenimler eklenir - genel best practice eklenmez
4. TONIVA girdileri kalici olarak arsiv dosyasinda
5. Yeni giris eklerken ilgili kategori basliginin altina ekle
| 2026-04-14 | Hangfire 1.8.14 RecurringJobScheduler'Ä± tamamen disable eden doÄŸrudan API yok (BackgroundJobServerOptions public toggle yok) | Ã‡alÄ±ÅŸan pattern: `options.SchedulePollingInterval = TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(int.MaxValue)` (~24.86 gÃ¼n, Hangfire setter cap'i). Worker process'leri 25 gÃ¼nden sÄ±k recycle edildiÄŸi iÃ§in scheduler pratikte tick atmaz. **NOT:** `TimeSpan.FromDays(36500)` (100 yÄ±l) **Ã‡ALIÅžMAZ** â€” setter `must be <= 2147483647 ms` ArgumentOutOfRangeException fÄ±rlatÄ±r, servis crash-loop'a girer. Leader/worker topology'de sadece Backend scheduler sahibi; worker'lar `recurring-jobs` advisory lock'u yarÄ±ÅŸmaz. Shared extension optional param ile (default=true â†’ backward compat) opt-in |
| 2026-04-14 | Codex PASS iter 0 aldÄ± ama production deploy'da 3 worker `SchedulePollingInterval must be non-negative and either equal to or less than 2147483647 milliseconds` exception'Ä± ile crash-loop'a girdi (ad508b1 â†’ hotfix) | **Codex static review runtime numeric bounds'Ä± otomatik doÄŸrulayamaz** â€” Ã¶zellikle 3rd-party library setter validation'larÄ± diff'ten gÃ¶rÃ¼nmez. Ã–ÄŸrenim: TimeSpan/int cast iÃ§eren setter'larda hedef kÃ¼tÃ¼phanenin kaynak kodunu / docs'unu manuel kontrol et; setter exception fÄ±rlatabiliyorsa CoVe sorusuna "setter cap"Ä± aÃ§Ä±kÃ§a yaz. Deploy-sonrasÄ± smoke log kontrolÃ¼ ZORUNLU (health OK â‰  process stable â€” worker'lar ilk request gelmeden Ã¶nce crash edebilir) |
| 2026-04-14 | Yeni paket build gate (`npm run build` PASS) talep ediyor ama tree'de pre-existing TS hatalari forbidden_areas (paket scope disi) icinde | **Sibling cleanup paketi pattern**: Pre-existing hatalari ayri kardes plan JSON ile temizle (LOW risk, davranis-degisikligi-sifir, sadece TS6133 unused + TS2322 type fix). Once cleanup paketini /rev + commit, sonra ana paketin /rev'ini tekrar dene. forbidden_areas kuralini ihlal etmek (paket scope'u genisletmek) veya `tsc --skipLibCheck` ile bypass etmek YASAK. Q'ya 4 secenek sun (skip codex / proceed annotate / sibling cleanup / scope expand) ve onay bekle |
| 2026-04-14 | Codex CQ1/CQ12 (error handling quality + INV-XX-NNN code policy) **frontend bridge/iframe modullerine de uygulanir** â€” backend-only saniliyor ama Codex internal Error.message'larini bile actionable + registered code talep ediyor | **Frontend bridge errors icin pattern**: (1) `arch/errors.md`'ye INV-INT-* (Integration namespace) kodlar ekle; (2) `inmaErrors.ts` benzeri sabit dosya `INMA_ERRORS = { CODE_NAME: 'INV-INT-NNN' } as const` + `inmaErrorMessage(code, detail?)` helper "{code}: {description} (detail)" formatinda; (3) tum Error/throw mesajlari helper'dan gecsin, code-only string ASLA kullanilmasin; (4) external (parent/server) kontrollu string'leri ERROR CODE olarak kullanma â€” sabit registered code'a normalize et + parent string'i sadece optional `detail` parametresi olarak sakla (CQ9 leak prevention) |
| 2026-04-14 | Debug/diagnostic UI component (InmaConnectionStatus benzeri) hardcoded EN label + raw error code expose ederse Codex CQ10 (UX consistency) FAIL | Debug component bile olsa: (1) Kullanici-yonelik etiketler **TR localized** (proje dilini takip et); (2) Raw error code (`INV-INT-*`) UI'da gosterilme â€” sadece `title` tooltip'inde dev/destek icin diagnostic; (3) `kod: ${error}` gibi diagnostic info olusturulurken `[apiBaseUrl, errorCode].filter(Boolean).join(' \| ')` pattern ile compose et |
| 2026-04-14 | Codex MCP review **stateless** â€” iter N'de evidence subset gondermek iter N-1'de PASS olmus alanlari UNKNOWN'a regress eder. Iter 2'de sadece `Verify()` snippet gondererek iter 1'de PASS olan SQL/repo/region evidence kayboldu, 12 CQ -> 6 UNKNOWN/FAIL. | **Her iter'da TUM kanit gomulu olmali**: (1) git_diff alanina kritik tum dosyalari embed et (sadece degisen kismi degil, ilgili tum metodlari); (2) onceki iter'larda PASS olan alanlari da yeni submission'a tasi (Codex memory yok); (3) iter N'de sadece N-1'in delta'sini gondermek YANLIS â€” full snapshot gerekli. Diff dosyasi (diff_file_path) yoksa iter `git_diff` field'inda ozet+kritik snippetlar olmali. |
| 2026-04-14 | Codex policy claim "JWT must be on all routes" mis-applied OAuth callback'lerinde (RFC 6749 Â§3.1.2 browser redirect Bearer header gonderemez) | Codex policy hard-rule'lari context-free uyguluyor. Cozumu: (1) Codebase pattern evidence'i submission'da explicit goster (`UseJwtAuth(jwtValidator, logger, "/api/v1/")` -> sadece /api/v1/ JWT-protected, mevcut public endpoint'ler /health /ready /api/ops/endpoints); (2) RFC referansi ekle; (3) State HMAC + TTL + FixedTimeEquals = OAuth-standard CSRF defense; (4) Iter 0'da explicit yazsan da Codex skip edebilir, iter 1'de policy karsi argumanini repeat et. |
| 2026-04-14 | OAuth state token icin JWT lib (System.IdentityModel.Tokens.Jwt) kullanmak overkill â€” algoritma confusion + key type bugs riski | Custom HMAC-SHA256 + base64url payload (`<payload>.<hmac>` format) yeterli. Avantaj: (1) Tek algoritma, sabit key type; (2) Attack surface kucuk; (3) Debug kolay; (4) Payload format ozellestirilebilir (`tenantId\|nonce\|iat\|exp`). Sart: `CryptographicOperations.FixedTimeEquals` timing-safe compare + secret>=32 byte enforcement. |
| 2026-04-14 | Lazy-on-demand token refresh + thundering herd korumasi: cache miss anliginda 100 paralel istek gelirse 100 Zoho refresh tetiklenir, rate limit (200 req/dk) asilir | **Per-tenant SemaphoreSlim + double-check pattern**: (a) Cache hit fast-path (no lock); (b) `ConcurrentDictionary<int, SemaphoreSlim>` ile per-tenant lock; (c) Lock alindiktan sonra cache'i tekrar kontrol (double-check) â€” bekleyen ilk istek refresh yaparken digerleri release sonrasi cache hit yasar; (d) `finally { gate.Release(); }` exception/cancellation guvenli. Sonuc: 100 paralel istek = 1 Zoho refresh. |
| 2026-04-14 | DataProtection IDataProtector encrypt edilmis veriler process restart sonrasi decrypt edilemiyorsa ortam kaybolmustur | `AddDataProtection().SetApplicationName("X").PersistKeysToFileSystem(new DirectoryInfo(path))` ZORUNLU: (1) ApplicationName sabit string (env'lerden bagimsiz); (2) PersistKeysToFileSystem path stable storage'da (C:\Invekto\Integrations\keys gibi servis-spesifik klasor, deploy_output disinda); (3) Klasor yoksa `Directory.CreateDirectory()` ile create et; (4) Backup stratejisi: key ring kaybi = tum encrypted veri okunamaz hale gelir, tenants reconnect zorunlu. |
| 2026-04-14 | DataProtection key ring servis startup'unda otomatik olusturuluyor (`Directory.CreateDirectory` + `PersistKeysToFileSystem`) â€” manuel klasor olusturma deploy adimi gereksiz | Deploy checklist'inden "key ring klasoru olustur" cikar, sadece servis hesabinin write izni olmasi yeterli. Ilk authorize cagrisinda `key-{guid}.xml` olusur. Backup stratejisi: bu klasor + plain DB backup yetmez, key ring kaybi = tum tenant reconnect. |

Dash KULLANMA. deploy.md tablosunda fix gerekir. mcp__invekto-ops__server-status enum'i zaten dashsiz ad bekliyor (ek dogrulama). |
| 2026-04-15 | Codex sabit-adi-okuyup-INV-XX-NNN-format-yok-dedi false positive â€” `ErrorCodes.GeneralValidation` aslinda `"INV-GEN-003"` constant'ina cozuluyor ama Codex constants dosyasini gormeden sadece kullanim satirina bakti | (1) Mini diff'lerde Codex'in goremedigi context (constants, enums, arch dosyalari) submission'a manuel dahil et ya da plan summary'sinde acikla; (2) Yine de iter 1'de service-namespaced dedicated kod (INV-OPS-001) eklemek defansif olarak dogru karar â€” mevcut kullanim spesifik degildi; (3) Genel kural: validation-spesifik durumlar icin GeneralValidation yerine domain-spesifik kod (orn. INV-OPS-001 quicklogin tenant invalid) daha okunabilir log + arch/errors.md doc actionable. |
| 2026-04-15 | Invekto.Shared/Constants/ErrorCodes.cs additive constant ekleme â€” yeni `public const string` icin TUM servisleri redeploy GEREKMEZ | Yeni constant existing servisler tarafindan referans edilmediginden eski compiled IL eski Shared.dll ile calismaya devam eder. Sadece YENI constant'i KULLANAN servis(ler) redeploy edilir. Bu mini paket sirasinda Backend dahil sadece 1 servis deploy yetti, diger 11 servis stable. Karsi durum: existing constant degeri DEGISIRSE/SILINIRSE break â€” o zaman tum servisler redeploy. |
| 2026-04-15 | Smoke testi yapilamadigi durumda paketi yine de prod'a deploy etmek dogru karar â€” feature flag (inmaAuthMockEnabled) ile gated oldugu icin exposure sifir | LOW risk + flag-gated + back-compat preserved icin "deploy-without-smoke" kabul edilebilir; smoke'u sonraki session/uygun zamana ertele. Gate olmasaydi (production'a aktif ekilen behavior degisikligi) DEPLOY ETME, smoke'u ZORUNLU say. Genel kural: deploy-go karari = blast_radius * exposure; flag-gated dormant kod = blast_radius~0. |
| 2026-04-15 | PostgreSQL atomic upsert with conflict on subset of rows requires PARTIAL UNIQUE INDEX matching ON CONFLICT WHERE clause exactly | `INSERT ... ON CONFLICT (cols) WHERE <pred> DO UPDATE` works only if a PARTIAL UNIQUE INDEX exists with the SAME `WHERE <pred>`. Adim 3 P1 sync-log: needed to allow re-attempt for (tenant,event,lead) in {pending,failed} but allow multiple historical 'success' rows. Solution: `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_zoho_sync_log_open_attempt ON zoho_sync_log(tenant_id,gunes_event,gunes_lead_id) WHERE status IN ('pending','failed')` + matching `ON CONFLICT (...) WHERE status IN ('pending','failed')`. This eliminates the read-then-write race in CTE-based upsert patterns. Pattern: when retry semantics need "one open attempt per business key, unlimited terminal records", use partial unique on the open-state subset. |
| 2026-04-15 | Codex'in "JWT all routes + tenant_id from JWT" hard-rule'unu spec_architectural_decisions ile override etmek mumkun DEGIL â€” Codex policy bunu non-negotiable sayar | Internal service-to-service endpoint'ler icin `/api/v1/` disinda mount + shared-secret + caller-supplied tenant kullanan tasarimlarda Codex CQ9/Q2 surekli FAIL doner. Q FORCE PASS gerekir. Cozumler: (a) Q FORCE PASS (Adim 3 P1'de yapildi, blast_radius=secret rotation), (b) per-service JWT minter infrastructure (buyuk scope), (c) HMAC-signed payload binding (orta scope). Adim 3'te Q (a)'yi sectim. **Kural:** internal endpoint tasarimi konusunda Q'ya interview'de sor ve Codex override beklentisini explicit say. |
| 2026-04-15 | Bare catch ve catch(Exception) PROJECT-WIDE FORBIDDEN â€” exception handling'de sadece typed catch listesi | Yasak: `catch { }` ve `catch (Exception ex)`. Izin verilen pattern: gerceklesebilecek exception turlerini ozellestir. Adim 3 P1'de typed catch listesi: `HttpRequestException` (transport), `System.IO.IOException` (IO), `TaskCanceledException` (timeout/cancel), `System.Text.Json.JsonException` (parse), `Npgsql.NpgsqlException` (DB). Best-effort body-read patterns icin de typed listesi kullan, bare catch ile suppress etme. Codex CQ2/CQ5 bunu hard-rule olarak okur. Service-layer `ArgumentException`'da INV-XX-NNN prefix ekle (`INV-GEN-003: ...`) â€” Codex CQ12 mesajda kod bekliyor. |
| 2026-04-15 | Internal service endpoint'leri /api/v1/ DISINDA mount edilirse JwtAuth middleware otomatik bypass eder | `UseJwtAuth(jwtValidator, logger, "/api/v1/")` middleware sadece /api/v1/ prefix'li route'larda calisir. Internal-only endpoint'i (POST /api/internal/zoho/sync) /api/v1/ disinda mount edersen JWT middleware path'i atlar; endpoint kendi auth handler'ini calistirmali (X-Internal-Service-Token check). Bu pattern WhatsAppAnalytics /api/ops/benchmark/* ile uyumlu. **Dikkat:** caller (Backend) tenant context'i body'de gondermek zorunda kalir â€” bu trust boundary acikca dokumente edilmeli (spec_architectural_decisions). |
| 2026-04-16 | Backend `UseJwtAuth` whitelist pattern: prefix yoksa JWT otomatik bypass edilir (Invekto.Backend; Integrations'tan farkli) | Backend `JwtAuthMiddleware` `authRequiredPrefixes` parametre listesi kullanir â€” her yeni `/api/v1/X/` grubu icin Program.cs'deki `app.UseJwtAuth(..., "/api/v1/webhook/", ...)` cagrisina prefix EKLENMELI. Aksi halde endpoint public kalir (tenant spoofing riski). Integrations'ta `UseJwtAuth(..., "/api/v1/")` tek-prefix opt-out pattern kullaniyor (ters model). **Checklist:** Backend'de yeni /api/v1/X/ endpoint'i eklemeden once UseJwtAuth listesini kontrol et. |
| 2026-04-16 | Codex CoVe "arch/errors.md user_message LITERAL match" hard-rule â€” semantic-uygun olsa bile farkli context'ler icin mesaj metni farkli ise FAIL doner | INV-INT-127 arch/errors.md entry'si P2 auto-retry context'i icin. P3-B1 UI proxy transport hatasi semantik olarak FARKLI (user-initiated, no auto-retry), actionable mesaj dogru. Ama Codex string-level karsilastirma yapiyor â†’ FAIL. **Cozumler:** (a) arch/errors.md'ye context-per-entry koda ayrik ekle (INV-INT-127a/127b), (b) proxy mesaji literal kopyasi olsun (UX bozar), (c) Q FORCE PASS (semantic-dogru; P1/P2/P3-A precedent). P3-B1'de (c) secildi. **Kural:** ayni error code'u birden fazla cagri context'inde kullaniyorsan ya arch/errors.md'yi generic yaz ya da sub-code'lar ac. |
| 2026-04-16 | Typed HttpClient + interface alias pattern â€” double HttpClient creation anti-pattern'i onler | **Yanlis:** `AddHttpClient<Impl>(cfg)` + `AddSingleton<IInterface>(sp => { var http = sp.GetRequiredService<IHttpClientFactory>().CreateClient(nameof(Impl)); ... return new Impl(http, ...); })` â€” IHttpClientFactory.CreateClient(nameof(Impl)) typed-client registration key ile ayni degil, yeni unconfigured client olusturur (Codex CQ5 FAIL). **Dogru:** `AddHttpClient<Impl>(cfg)` + `AddTransient<IInterface>(sp => sp.GetRequiredService<Impl>())` â€” typed-client otomatik DI injection, alias interface'i ayni instance'a baglar. Kural: `AddHttpClient<T>()` kullaninca T'yi dogrudan resolve et, IHttpClientFactory.CreateClient(nameof(T)) cagirma. |

| 2026-04-17 | **Silent failure iki cesittir â€” silent reject < silent success. Silent success TEHLIKELI.** runInmaExchange 401'de `try/catch + console.warn + return` yapiyordu; caller `await` hatasiz resolve gorup `bootstrap.run` 'SUCCESS' logluyor + `state='authenticated'` + `isAuthenticated:true`. Ama raw INMA JWT localStorage'da stale â€” sonraki her API call INV-AUTH-002 Invalid token signature 401. Log'lar YALAN soyluyor, teshis 20dk aldi. | **Silent reject vs silent success ayrimi kritik:** (a) Silent reject (lesson 535) = early-return + hicbir log = teshisi yavaslatir ama downstream state bozulmaz. (b) Silent success = try/catch swallow + `return` (no throw) = caller hatasiz sanir, downstream bozuk state ile devam eder. **Kural:** Async auth/exchange/token path'lerinde asla `catch { console.warn(...); return; }` yazma. Iki secenek: (1) `throw new Error('... HTTP ${status}')` â€” caller catch eder + cleanup yapar; (2) explicit sentinel return (`Promise<boolean>` veya `Promise<Result>`) â€” caller false/error branch kontrol eder. Silent `return` (void swallow) YASAK. Caller tarafinda da await sonrasi state'i dogrula: basari logluyorsan `storeTokens` cagrildi mi kontrol et (side-effect evidence). Applied: runInmaExchange -> throw, executeWithRefresh try/catch + console.warn (fall-through preserved, bos degil), useAuth useEffect'lere `.catch` eklendi. |

| 2026-04-17 | Lazy auto-provision race-safe pattern: INSERT nextval ON CONFLICT (col) WHERE predicate DO NOTHING RETURNING + re-SELECT fallback | Multi-tenant lazy provisioning (unknown external id -> yeni internal id) icin race-safe pattern: (a) Fast-path `SELECT tenant_id FROM tenant_registry WHERE inma_code=@code LIMIT 1` (hit = return); (b) Insert-path `INSERT INTO tenant_registry(tenant_id, ...) VALUES (nextval('tenant_registry_auto_id_seq'), ...) ON CONFLICT (inma_code) WHERE inma_code IS NOT NULL DO NOTHING RETURNING tenant_id` (parallel first-login: sadece biri eklenir, loser RETURNING bos); (c) Race fallback `SELECT tenant_id FROM tenant_registry WHERE inma_code=@code LIMIT 1` (loser winner'in tenant_id'sini okur). **Kritik:** `ON CONFLICT ON CONSTRAINT <index_name>` SADECE UNIQUE/EXCLUDE CONSTRAINT icin calisir, `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX` (standalone) icin DEGIL â€” inference clause kullan: `ON CONFLICT (col) WHERE predicate` (partial predicate index matches). Codex CQ5/CQ11 bu semantic farki hard-rule olarak yakalar. **Kural:** (1) Partial unique index icin ON CONFLICT inference (predicate match), ON CONSTRAINT degil; (2) nextval() wasted sequence value paralel conflict'te kabul edilir (counter sabit artar, gap olusur â€” sorun degil); (3) re-SELECT fallback zorunlu (RETURNING bos donerse winner'i bul); (4) Impossible race (SELECT re-miss) icin typed `InvalidOperationException` throw. Multi-tenant onboarding friction'i 0'a indirir â€” INMA'da Company olustur + kullanici login = INSE kendini provision eder (migration 009 NOTE'unda planlanmisti, UP0.3). |

| 2026-04-19 | Same-transaction audit atomicity â€” pre-lookup + UPDATE + audit INSERT ayni tx boundary icinde; "ayni connection ama farkli tx" YETERLI DEGIL | FEAT-LIW Chunk C iter 1 FAIL (CQ9): Plan'da LiwSettingsService mutation methods oncu FindByTenantIdAsync (kendi conn acar) cagirip sonra BeginTransactionAsync'te UPDATE+audit yapiyordu. Codex: "before-snapshot outside transaction produces stale audit under concurrent writer". Dogru: optimistic concurrency WHERE updated_at=@expected UPDATE zero rows donderiyorsa audit zaten rollback oluyor. AMA structural guarantee (same-tx) Codex'in argument surface area'sini kapatir. Iter 2 fix: TenantLandingSettingsRepository.FindByTenantIdAsync gained NpgsqlConnection overload; LiwSettingsService Rotate/Revoke/UpdateFieldMap restructured: Open conn + BEGIN tx FIRST, then in-tx FindByTenantIdAsync(conn) + UPDATE + audit INSERT + COMMIT. Rotate first-time vs existing dallarini RotateFirstTimeInTxAsync + RotateExistingInTxAsync helpers'ina cikardim (disardaki public RotateApiKeyAsync tek tx acip path dispatch yapar). **Kural:** Audit trail yazan mutation methods icin pre-lookup DAIMA transaction icinde olsun. Pattern: `await using var conn = await _db.OpenConnectionAsync(ct); await using var tx = await conn.BeginTransactionAsync(ct); try { var before = await _repo.FindByIdAsync(conn, id, ct); ... await _repo.UpdateAsync(conn, ..., ct); ... await _auditRepo.InsertAsync(conn, ..., ct); await tx.CommitAsync(ct); } catch (NpgsqlException ex) { rollback }`. Repo methods `NpgsqlConnection conn` parametre overload'una ihtiyac duyar (mevcut parameterless API kirilmaz). Same-connection + same-tx guarantee audit-settings drift'i structural olarak onler, optimistic concurrency'nin korektlik argument'una bagli kalma. |
| 2026-04-19 | Dirty-state preservation on 409 refetch â€” alignedRowVersionRef useRef pattern separates clean-sync vs drift-detected; dirty rows asla silently overwrite edilmez | FEAT-LIW Chunk C iter 0 FAIL (CQ10+Q2): Plan AC10 "preserve dirty rows on 409 refetch + overwrite clean state" diyordu ama FieldMapEditorCard useEffect [settings.updated_at]'a bagliydi VE UNCONDITIONALLY rows = rowsFromSettings(settings) setliyordu. 409 refetch sonrasi settings.updated_at degisti mi dirty rows silindi. Iter 1 fix: alignedRowVersionRef useRef tutuldu (son-aligned server updated_at); useEffect'te if (alignedRowVersionRef.current === serverRowVersion) return; sonra if (dirty) setServerDrifted(true); else resetFromSettings(); alignedRowVersionRef.current = serverRowVersion. Banner "Sunucu tarafinda degisiklik tespit edildi" + explicit acceptServerState() butonu (kullanici "degisikliklerimi goz ardi et" derse). **Kural:** Dashboard mutation + optimistic concurrency pattern'lerinde local dirty state AUTOMATIC refetch ile asla uzerine yazilmasin. useRef ile "last-aligned" row_version tutarak clean-path vs drift-path ayrilmali. Clean-path = initial load + post-successful-save refetch (dirty=false garantili cunku save dirty clear eder); drift-path = 409 refetch (dirty=true olabilir). Drift'te banner + accept button, save retry'de mevcut dirty rows tekrar submit (expected_row_version refreshed). Alternatif "field-by-field merge" (local-dirty + server-new akilli birlestirme) Codex'in sordugu daha ileri pattern ama YAGNI â€” banner + explicit accept Chunk C icin yeterli. |
| 2026-04-19 | 400 vs 409 semantic â€” missing required concurrency token (If-Match / expected_row_version) CLIENT INPUT BUG, concurrency conflict DEGIL | FEAT-LIW Chunk C iter 2 FAIL (CQ1+CQ9+CQ12): Plan'da LiwSettingsService.UpdateFieldMapAsync missing ExpectedRowVersion icin Conflict() (409 INV-BE-113 "Ayarlar baska bir sekmede degistirildi") doniyordu. Codex: bu CLIENT INPUT omission â€” bad request (400), not concurrency conflict. Iter 3 fix: ValidationFailed(new ValidationError(SourceField="expected_row_version", ErrorCode=INV-GEN-003, Message="If-Match header veya body.expected_row_version zorunlu (revoke/save oncesi GET /settings ile row_version alinmali).")) â€” endpoint layer 400 + errors[] envelope. **Kural:** Optimistic concurrency endpoints'te HTTP status semantics disiplini: (a) 400 Validation â€” client required header/field'i atladi (If-Match yok, body.expected_row_version null) â†’ INV-GEN-003 veya similar validation code + actionable message. (b) 409 Conflict â€” client header/field gonderdi AMA sunucudaki current row_version ile eslesmedi (UPDATE WHERE updated_at=@expected 0 rows) â†’ INV-BE-113 "degistirildi, son hali yuklendi". Missing token â‰  mismatch. Endpoint cliente "ne atladin" (400) vs "baska biri onden yetisti" (409) actionable farki korumali. Benzer pattern Revoke endpoint'i: missing If-Match â†’ 400, unparseable date â†’ 400, mismatch (UPDATE 0 rows) â†’ 409. |
| 2026-04-19 | Typed catch rule TS tarafinda da gecerli â€” bare `catch {}` CQ5 FAIL; `catch (err)` + instanceof narrowing zorunlu | FEAT-LIW Chunk C iter 1+2 FAIL (CQ5): Plan'da 3 TS component (DryRunPreviewCard + FieldMapEditorCard paste helper + NewKeyCopyModal clipboard handler) bare `catch { setError("..."); }` kullanildi. Iter 1 fix 2 component'i duzeltti (SyntaxError instanceof narrowing ile parse-error mesaji), iter 2'de NewKeyCopyModal'i atladim â€” iter 2 CQ5 FAIL yeniden cikti. Iter 3 fix: NewKeyCopyModal catch (err) { const reason = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "unknown"; console.warn(`[LIW-FE-CLIP] ... (${reason})`); setCopyFailed(true); }. **Kural:** TypeScript TRY/CATCH'te bare catch block `{}` ASLA yazilmaz. Her zaman `catch (err)` + `instanceof` narrowing + optional diagnostic console.warn/log ekle. Pattern: `catch (err) { const detail = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err); console.warn("[COMPONENT-PREFIX] context: " + detail); /* fallback UX */ }`. Project CQ5 rule "Typed catch blocks ONLY" sadece C# icin degil â€” TypeScript diff'inde de Codex bare catch flag'liyor. Diff'i gozden gecirirken her `catch` blogunu scan et: `catch (` parentheses var mi? Yoksa fix. IDE search regex: `catch\s*\{` (space/newline bare catch). C# tarafinda ise typed exception zorunlu + pattern `catch (OperationCanceledException) when (ct.IsCancellationRequested) { throw; }` = caller cancellation semantics preservation (silently null'a converting YASAK, rethrow zorunlu). |
| 2026-04-19 | FK violation misclassification â€” generic NpgsqlException catch-all 23503 FK detail'i yutuyor, actionable 400 yerine 503 DB-001 donuyor | FEAT-LIW Chunk C PROD smoke step 5 (tenant_id=9999 rotate): /rotate first-time â†’ 503 INV-DB-001 "Veritabani baglantisi kurulamadi." | 2026-04-20 | Postgres IMMUTABLE expression index â€” date_trunc / extract(epoch) on TIMESTAMPTZ is STABLE, not IMMUTABLE; STORED generated columns still fail same check | FEAT-J2 migration 017 prod execute initial attempt: `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uq_inma_optout_outbox_dedup ON inma_optout_outbox (tenant_id, phone, event_type, date_trunc('second', created_at));` â†’ FAIL "functions in index expression must be marked IMMUTABLE". Tried `date_trunc('second', created_at, 'UTC')` (PG16+ syntax, not available on prod); tried STORED generated column `created_at_second TIMESTAMPTZ GENERATED ALWAYS AS (date_trunc('second', created_at)) STORED` â†’ same FAIL because STORED generation expr requires IMMUTABLE; tried `floor(extract(epoch from created_at))::BIGINT` â†’ same FAIL (extract(epoch from timestamptz) is STABLE, timezone-aware). **Root cause:** Postgres classifies ANY function operating on `TIMESTAMPTZ` with timezone-dependent behavior as STABLE. The `datestyle`/`TimeZone` session setting can change results â†’ not IMMUTABLE. Even STORED generated columns check the expression at column-definition time with the same rule. **Resolution:** Removed DB-layer dedup. Repository `INSERT INTO outbox RETURNING id` (no ON CONFLICT). Duplicate enqueues from webhook retry storm handled downstream: drain job pushes both rows, INMA returns `StatusCode='909' AlreadyOptedOut` on the second, MarkOutboxProcessed normalizes. Net effect: fuller audit trail, no user-visible duplicates, no lost work. **Kural:** TIMESTAMPTZ Ã¼zerinde second-granularity dedup/bucket index gereken her yerde: (a) date_trunc ve extract(epoch) IMMUTABLE DEÄžÄ°L, unique index'te kullanÄ±lamaz; (b) STORED generated columns bu constraint'i bypass etmez, generation expression de IMMUTABLE olmalÄ±; (c) Workaround seÃ§enekleri: downstream idempotency (INMA 909 pattern, if API provides), application-layer SELECT-then-INSERT race-tolerant, or migrate column type to TIMESTAMP WITHOUT TIME ZONE (losing tz info). (d) PG16+'da `date_trunc(field, ts, 'UTC')` 3-argument form IMMUTABLE olarak iÅŸaretli â€” ama prod PG version check ÅŸart. Migration prototiplemeden Ã¶nce prod PG version + pg_proc immutability check: `SELECT provolatile FROM pg_proc WHERE proname = 'date_trunc'` ('i'=immutable, 's'=stable, 'v'=volatile). |

Kategori prefix mantigi: kart kategori sabit (migration seed) + prefix anlam tasiyor (C=Customer, K=Karar, O=Ops, D=Dev, U=UI, X=Doc) â€” koda bakinca tipi anla. Her iki yerde de visual-weight ayni ama anlam okumadan netlesti. **Kural:** Status/severity indicator'lerinde noktasal sembol yerine 1-2 karakter text label tercih et (P0/P1, !, âš , vs) â€” okumadan icerik anlasilsin. Tooltip ek bilgi katmani (bu kart icin "Pilot blocker - gecikirse pilot kayar" full sentence), ana semantik labelde olmali. Counter-pattern: minimalism dogmasi pahasina anlasilirligi feda etmek (Q tooltip'i okumadi, mobile users hover yapamaz, screen reader'lar dot'u "round graphic" der). Lesson kaynak: Q acik soru "kirmizi nokta nedir?" iter1 sonrasi. Pattern adi: "text-label > pure-symbol icin status indicator". |
| 2026-04-28 | Dependency CSV TEXT field â€” partial UNIQUE + regex CHECK ile array-tablo overhead'i olmadan iliski tutmak | FEAT-ROADMAP-V3-DEPENDS Migration 037'de kart bagimliliklari 2 secenek: (a) `depends_on TEXT[]` Postgres array (native, INDEXED via GIN, ANY/ALL operators); (b) `depends_on TEXT NULL` CSV string (basit, chk regex enforce, frontend split). Q istegi "kucuk bir feature" â€” 13 kartta 1-3 dependency, max 3 ref_code per row. Karar (b) CSV: hicbir CRUD endpoint cardinality query yapmiyor (UI sadece string'i split edip render); array overhead, casting, indexing gereksiz. CHECK constraint `depends_on IS NULL OR depends_on ~ '^[A-Z][0-9]{3}(,[A-Z][0-9]{3})*\$'` regex format enforce â€” runtime parse fail riski sifir. Frontend `card.depends_on?.split(',').map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean)` 1-line. **Kural:** Cardinality < 5 + read-only display + cross-table relation YOK = TEXT CSV + regex CHECK > array column. Counter-case: 100+ dependency, GIN index ile WHERE 'X001' = ANY(deps) gerekirse, array column degerli. YapÄ±sal liability: future relationship table (kanban_card_dependencies (card_id, depends_card_id) FK) gerekirse migration ile gec; baslangicta TEXT CSV tek kolon hizinda. Pattern adi: "CSV-with-regex-CHECK kucum dependency". |

Lesson 2026-04-28 mevcut entry (FEAT-PILOT-KANBAN iter2) bu pattern'i kayit altina almisti, FEAT-ROADMAP-V3-DEPENDS deploy'unda bilincli olarak `dotnet build 2>&1 \| grep -iE "error CS\|0 Error\|Build succeeded"` kullandim, sonuc temiz "Build succeeded. 0 Error(s)" cikti. Tek `sql` parametre icinde BEGIN; DO $precondition$ ... $; CREATE TABLE ...; INSERT ...; ALTER TABLE...; UPDATE ...; DO $update_welcome$ ... $; DO $verify$ ... $; COMMIT; sirasi gonderildi. Return: 'UNKNOWN OK â€” 0 row(s) affected'. Bu mesaj multi-statement transaction'lar icin TYPICAL â€” postgres son statement'in (COMMIT) row count'unu donduruyor. Success kanit: post-state SELECT verify (column_exists + row counts + JSONB structure). **Kural:** mcp__invekto-postgres__execute karmasik migration icin guvenilir, ama return mesaji yetersiz feedback â€” atomic execute sonrasi MUTLAKA verify SELECT yap (column existence + row counts + sample data). RAISE EXCEPTION trigger oldugunda postgres exception throw edip transaction abort eder, MCP error doner â€” 'UNKNOWN OK' demek BEGIN..COMMIT akisi tamamlandi (postcondition DO $verify$ blocku da PASS, RAISE EXCEPTION yok). **Idempotent re-run safe:** ALTER TABLE IF NOT EXISTS + UPDATE WHERE LIKE-presence-check + DO $update_welcome$ '%placeholder%' early-return + archive table CREATE IF NOT EXISTS â€” re-run ROW_COUNT=0 fail-loud DEGIL, normal flow. |

| 2026-06-08 | Broadcast counter FUNNEL (+1) degil PARTITION (yeni bucket +1, canli eski bucket GREATEST-1) -- delivery transition'da invariant korunur; FOR UPDATE ile canli eski status | cxapi PR-3b-2: mevcut `IncrementBroadcastCounterAsync` funnel-style (sadece +1, queued-1 sent/failed'de) idi -- prod'da CALISIYOR gibiydi cunku delivery ack'leri hic correlate olmuyordu (ext_id NULL her satirda, mig 058). Ack'ler inince `sent+delivered+read+failed+ambiguous+queued=total_recipients` invariant'i kirilir (count > total, %>100). **Fix:** Yeni atomic `ApplyDeliveryStatusAsync` (tek CTE, `MarkCxapiOutcomeAsync` deseni) transition'da yeni bucket +1 VE eski bucket GREATEST(-1,0); eski bucket'i CANLI okumak icin `FOR UPDATE` (concurrent/out-of-order ack'ler serialize, snapshot-stale double-count engellenir) + monotonic downgrade guard (sent<delivered<read; failed sadece queued/sending/posting/sent uzerine). Duplicate/regression → applied=false no-op (counter oynamaz). **Kural:** Bir aggregate counter "her durum tek bucket" partition invariant'i belgeliyorsa, durum gecisinde MUTLAKA eski bucket'i decrement et (sadece +1 = funnel = invariant kirar); concurrent/out-of-order event'lerde gecis-oncesi durumu `FOR UPDATE` ile canli oku, snapshot CTE degeri stale double-count yapar. |

Q'ya getirdim -> "status self-heal ekle". iter9 PASS 12/12+6/6. **Kural:** (a) finally bir CATCH degildir; lifecycle-invariant icin finally-backstop + finalized-flag broad-catch yasagini ihlal etmeden tum exception tiplerini kapsar, (b) cleanup/recovery path never-throw olmali (typed catch + log, asla rethrow -> orijinal exception'i maskelemesin), (c) durable state guarantee'leri write-path'te tam saglanamaz (DB-down) -> read-path self-heal (idempotent, sonraki okumada duzelt) tamamlayicidir, (d) Codex kendiyle-celisen/imkansiz talebe girince DUR, structural cozum (read-time reconciliation) uret veya Q'ya escalate et, sonsuz iter'leme. Codex flagged that connection/transient driver failures surface as `NpgsqlException` (the base class) and would escape as opaque 500s. **Rule:** in repository/service typed-catch blocks that map DB errors to INV codes, catch `NpgsqlException` (covers PostgresException + connection/timeout/transient). `PostgresException` only for SqlState-specific handling (e.g. UniqueViolation). Also: every DB-touching service method needs the catch (List/Create/Update/Delete/probe/import + upfront lookups + connection-open inside the try), not just the obvious write path. Repeatable across all Npgsql repos. |

The /rev diff (`git diff --cached`) showed PATCH → Codex CQ8 "method mismatch" false-looking blocker. Root cause = staging, not code. **Rule:** after editing any file for a review, `git add` it again immediately; before submitting /rev, spot-check `git show :path` (staged) for the key change, not just the working tree. |
| 2026-06-03 | Codex asserts "project rules" that may not exist — verify against codebase prevalence + review-policy.md, then reject false positives with evidence (don't blindly comply) | FEAT-OBI Phase 1A: Codex repeatedly FAILed on (a) "no SQL string composition" for `$@"...{constFragment}"` and (b) "use only 400/401/403/404/500" for 409/422. Both are NOT real project rules: interpolated SQL with STATIC const fragments (all user VALUES parameterized) is used in 9 repos / 46 occurrences (`grep -rho '\$@"' src/**/*.cs | wc -l`); 409/422 are established (pre-existing OutboundCampaign maps 409). review-policy.md only says "Parameterized query + sync". **Rule:** when Codex cites a "non-negotiable rule," grep the codebase for prevalence + check review-policy.md/INVEKTO_BASE before refactoring. If it's an established pattern + the actual rule is satisfied, document the rejection in the plan verdict's `rejected_findings` and (with Q permission) FORCE PASS rather than churn. Genuine violations (null-forgiving `!`, silent catch w/o log) DO get fixed. |
| 2026-06-03 | "Done + staged" backend from a prior session is NOT review-verified — run the holistic Codex review before trusting it | FEAT-OBI Phase 1A: GR1-3 backend was declared "BİTTİ + staged" (commit withheld per Codex-PASS rule). The first holistic /rev (covering staged backend + new frontend) found REAL defects in the "done" backend: counter-recompute subqueries missing tenant_id, null-forgiving `!`, no status lifecycle, invalid-row/error-mapping gaps, a preview cap-truncation race. **Rule:** staged-but-unreviewed code is unverified; the Codex gate applies to the WHOLE diff, and a prior session's "done" claim doesn't substitute for it. Budget for backend fixes even when your task is "just the frontend." |
| 2026-06-03 | Client-side CSV parsing must decode UTF-8 explicitly — SheetJS guesses Windows-1252 for a BOM-less CSV and mojibakes Turkish | FEAT-OBI Phase 1A live test: imported CSV names corrupted "Yılmaz"->"YÄ±lmaz", "Ayşe"->"AyÅe" — and the garbage PERSISTED to list_records (not just display). Root cause: `XLSX.read(arrayBuffer, {type:'array'})` for a BOM-less CSV defaults to codepage 1252. **Rule:** for CSV, decode bytes yourself: `XLSX.read(new TextDecoder('utf-8').decode(buf), {type:'string'})` (TextDecoder strips BOM + forces UTF-8). Excel (.xlsx/.xls) keeps `type:'array'` (text is UTF-8 internally). For a Turkish product, always test ı/ş/ç/ğ/ü/ö round-trip through any client-side file parser. Cross-project (any SheetJS/CSV upload). |
| 2026-06-03 | Dashboard ApiClientError must read BOTH `errorCode` (camel) and `error_code` (snake) — services serialize camelCase | FEAT-OBI: a 403 showed "UNKNOWN: ..." because `ErrorResponse` (ErrorCode/Message, no JsonPropertyName) serializes camelCase via ASP.NET default, but `api.ts ApiClientError` only read snake_case `error_code`. **Rule:** `errBody?.error_code ?? errBody?.errorCode ?? 'UNKNOWN'` (+ request_id/requestId). Also: a 403 feature-gate should render a calm "not enabled" state, not a red "please retry" — retry never helps a permission/feature gate. |
| 2026-06-03 | vite does NOT empty an outDir outside its root (wwwroot/app) — stale lazy chunks accumulate | Invekto Dashboard builds to `../wwwroot/app`; vite skips emptyOutDir for out-of-root dirs, so every build leaves old `DataImportPage-<hash>.js` chunks behind (harmless dead files — index.html references only the current entry bundle, which imports the current chunk). Not a deploy bug; server-deploy "cleans directory" before extract so prod stays consistent with the latest publish. If repo bloat matters, clean wwwroot/app/assets before build. Verify deploys via the chunk CHAIN (index.html -> index-*.js -> lazy chunk), not by grepping index.html for a lazy chunk (lazy chunks aren't referenced there). |
| 2026-06-03 | Compliance audit rows must be AUDIT-BEFORE-SERVE with a THROWING insert — best-effort-after-serve is a silent gap | FEAT-OBI Plan B (Export Manager, KVKK export_logs): iter-0 Codex FAILed because the audit insert was best-effort (catch+log+return false) and ran AFTER streaming the file — so a successful PII export could leave no audit row. **Rule:** for any "every X writes an audit row" invariant, build the payload first (in-memory, bounded), then write the audit row with an insert that THROWS on failure, and only THEN return the bytes/data. If the audit can't be written → fail the request (INV-OB-057), never serve. A separate best-effort `TryInsertFailureLog` is fine inside catch blocks (the op already failed). Corollary: prefer bounded in-memory build over streaming-from-DB-into-the-live-response when each response must be auditable/error-mappable — once the first byte is sent you can't map a mid-stream DB error to a clean code or guarantee the audit. Cross-project (any audited egress/export). |
| 2026-06-03 | PostgreSQL COUNT(*)/COUNT FILTER is bigint — reader.GetInt32 throws InvalidCastException; cast ::int or use GetInt64 | FEAT-OBI Plan B: report-data path read `SELECT COUNT(*) FILTER(...)` columns with `reader.GetInt32(n)` → runtime InvalidCastException (int8≠int4), and the surrounding catch only handled NpgsqlException so it bypassed INV-OB mapping into an unstructured 500. Note `Convert.ToInt32(ExecuteScalarAsync(...))` is SAFE (boxed-long unboxing handles it) — only `NpgsqlDataReader.GetInt32` on an aggregate is the trap. **Rule:** cast aggregate counts to `::int` in SQL (when bounded, e.g. a 50k cap) or read with `GetInt64`. Cross-project (any Npgsql COUNT into an Int32). |
| 2026-06-03 | Cap/fallback contract must be consistent — don't apply the same limit to the format you advertise as the unbounded fallback | FEAT-OBI Plan B: I capped BOTH CSV and XLSX at MaxXlsxRows while the INV-OB-058 message + UI told users "use CSV for too-large data" — a dead-end (CSV was also rejected). Codex FAILed CQ9/CQ10/CQ12. **Rule:** if the user-facing guidance/error names a fallback format/path for an over-limit case, that fallback must genuinely be unbounded (or have a strictly higher ceiling). Make the gate format-specific (`format == xlsx`), and keep the error message, arch/errors.md, and the SPA string all agreeing. Code-specific error messages (one per INV code) beat a single generic "export failed" per endpoint. |
| 2026-06-03 | PowerShell here-string (`@'...'@`) syntax in the BASH tool mangles the payload — bash parses `@'...'@` as `@` + single-quoted string + `@`, leaking literal `@` into the commit subject | FlowListPage deploy: ran `git commit -m @'...'@` via the **Bash** tool → subject became `@ feat(...)` and message tail had a trailing `@`. The `@'...'@` here-string is a **PowerShell** construct; bash has no idea. **Rule:** in the Bash tool use bash heredoc (`-F <(cat <<'EOF' ... EOF)`) or multiple `-m` flags; reserve `@'...'@` for the PowerShell tool only. Verify with `git log -1 --format='%s'` after committing; fix a bad subject with `git commit --amend` BEFORE pushing. Cross-project (any commit/multiline payload via the wrong shell tool). |
| 2026-06-03 | Two concurrent Claude sessions in the SAME repo dir can deploy the same services in the same minute — check `git fetch` + remote HEAD + prod artifact/flag state before assuming you're alone | FlowListPage deploy collided with a concurrent FEAT-OBI Plan B deploy session (both touched Backend+Outbound+migration053 at ~21:20). Surprise surfaced at /wrap: a `chore(wrap)` commit (`e5672fc4`) I never authored sat on top of my commit, already pushed; the other session had also ENABLED the Export flag for all tenants (contradicting my "flag OFF" statement to Q). They converged (same artifacts, 10/10 healthy, my SPA asset intact) — but only by luck of identical builds. **Rule:** (a) before deploy, `git fetch` + compare local vs origin HEAD to detect a parallel session; (b) after deploy, verify the LIVE prod artifact hash + any feature-flag/config the other session might have changed (don't trust your own pre-deploy assumption); (c) at /wrap, reconcile `origin/master...HEAD` and read any unexpected commit before committing more; (d) prefer the `invs` worktree-per-session launcher so parallel sessions don't share one working dir. Cross-project (any multi-session repo). |
| 2026-06-03 | A multi-write op's success audit row must be written INSIDE the same transaction as the write — a post-commit audit insert is a partial-success failure mode (write commits, audit throws → caller told it failed but the side effect persists) | FEAT-OBI Phase 1B create-list ("Liste Oluştur" → new data_list + list_records, then export_logs audit): iter1 Codex FAILed CQ2/Q3 because `CreateListFromFilterAsync` committed the list, THEN the service wrote the success audit on a separate connection — if that insert threw, the list existed but the response was INV-OB-057. **Fix:** gave `InsertLogCoreAsync` an optional `NpgsqlTransaction` param and wrote the success row INSIDE the create tx (before commit) → list + audit commit/rollback atomically. **Rule:** for "every committed X has an audit row," the audit insert shares X's transaction; only standalone/best-effort failure-audits run on their own connection. Read-only exports differ (audit-before-serve, no side effect to bind). Cross-project (any audited state-changing op). |
| 2026-06-03 | "Returns a specific error code" ≠ "not a silent failure" — if there's an audit-table contract, EVERY refused attempt (gate/validation/cap/conflict/empty) must write a status=failed audit row, not just the DB-error path | FEAT-OBI Phase 1B: iter0 Codex FAILed CQ2/CQ12/Q4 — `ExportFilteredAsync`/`CreateListFromExportAsync` returned INV-OB-056/058/059/060/061 on gate/validation/cap/conflict/empty WITHOUT a failed export_logs row (only NpgsqlException paths logged). I'd also over-promised this in the plan's Q4/error_handling, so Codex held me to my own stated contract. **Fix:** a local `FailAsync(code)` helper routes EVERY failure return through `TryInsertFailureLogAsync` (best-effort) before returning; the bad-format case clamps the logged `format` to a CHECK-valid value so the audit insert can't violate the constraint. **Rule:** (a) if you write "every X writes an audit row" in the plan, implement it on every branch — Codex enforces your stated contract literally; (b) pre-flight read-only endpoints touching no PII (count/options/history) intentionally DON'T audit (count fires per-keystroke → spam) — state that explicitly. Cross-project (any audited op with early-return rejections). |
| 2026-06-03 | Non-egress audit actions need HONEST enum values — widen the CHECK to add e.g. format='list'/delivery_mode='internal' rather than logging a list-creation as a fake csv/server_stream | FEAT-OBI Phase 1B: "Liste Oluştur" is PII duplication into an internal list, not file egress. Logging it as format='csv'/delivery_mode='server_stream' would be dishonest (audit-honesty was a Plan B Codex hot-button). **Fix:** migration 054 widened `chk_export_log_format` (+'list') and `chk_export_log_delivery` (+'internal'), added `ExportFormats.List`/`ExportDeliveryModes.Internal` consts, logged the create honestly. CHECK widening is additive (DROP IF EXISTS + ADD; existing rows stay valid since the value set only grows). **Rule:** audit enum values must describe what actually happened; if no existing value fits, widen the CHECK (additive, idempotent) instead of overloading a misleading one. Cross-project (any audit/event-log with a CHECK-constrained type/mode). |
| 2026-06-03 | Codex review policy: ROUTING changes auto-escalate LOW→MEDIUM, and a MEDIUM review's verification_questions must span the Data + Auth categories or CoVe FAILs on coverage | FEAT-OBI Veri Yönetimi tabs (pure frontend route merge): code was clean from iter0 (all CQ1-12 + CoVe PASS) but the review FAILed twice on POLICY only — iter0 "routing change must be MEDIUM not LOW + needs ≥3 verification questions" (I'd marked LOW with zero), iter1 "MEDIUM questions don't cover Data + Auth" (I had Process/Policy + Lifecycle). iter2 PASS after reclassifying MEDIUM + adding an Auth question (route stays behind ProtectedRoute) + a Data question (inner pages' tenant-scoped fetching unchanged). **Rule:** (a) any App.tsx route / nav-structure change → classify MEDIUM; (b) for MEDIUM, write verification questions covering all four categories (Auth/Data/Lifecycle/Process-Policy) even for frontend-only (Auth = "still gated?", Data = "fetching unchanged?"); (c) a genuinely-trivial follow-up (label text + element removal, no routing) can stay LOW and passes iter0. | 2026-06-07 | Junction-insert validated by a NON-locking pre-check (SELECT COUNT ... WHERE deleted_at IS NULL, then INSERT) has a soft-delete TOCTOU race under READ COMMITTED — a concurrent soft-delete (UPDATE deleted_at) can commit between the read and the parent tx commit, leaving a child row pointing at a now-deleted parent (the composite FK only checks row existence, not deleted_at) | PKT-14 Projeler S2 (project_targets -> data_lists): Codex iter1/iter2 flagged that AllTargetsValidAsync(COUNT) + validating-INSERT(JOIN deleted_at) still races a concurrent soft-delete. Fix: validate AND row-lock the parent rows with `SELECT id ... FOR SHARE` BEFORE any write, inside the tx — the lock holds until commit so a concurrent soft-delete BLOCKS. **FOR SHARE (not FOR KEY SHARE):** a soft-delete is a non-key UPDATE of deleted_at; FOR KEY SHARE only blocks key-column changes/DELETE so it would NOT block it; FOR SHARE blocks any concurrent UPDATE. | **Kural:** when a junction/child insert must reject a soft-deleted parent, a non-locking pre-check + a JOIN-on-deleted_at insert is NOT race-free under READ COMMITTED. Lock the parent rows `FOR SHARE` in the same tx before writing (FOR SHARE, not FOR KEY SHARE, because soft-delete = non-key UPDATE). Keep the validating INSERT + row-count check as the atomic backstop. Cross-project: any soft-delete + composite-FK junction write. |
| 2026-06-07 | Mapping a transactional repo's `tx.CommitAsync()` NpgsqlException to a "rolled back, safe to retry" error code is an OVERCLAIM — a COMMIT-phase transport failure leaves the commit outcome AMBIGUOUS (server may have committed after the client lost the connection) | PKT-14 Projeler S2: Codex iter3 CQ1/CQ12 — INV-OB-071 doc said "change rolled back (no partial write), safe to retry" but the service catch wraps CommitAsync too. Fix was DOC honesty (not special-casing commit exceptions — that would be cross-cutting + inconsistent with sibling ContactList INV-OB-053): reworded INV-OB-071 to "normally rolled back; a COMMIT-phase failure is ambiguous but a RETRY is still safe — create is unique-name-guarded (a retry after a hidden commit surfaces the conflict code, never a silent duplicate) and update/archive are idempotent." | **Kural:** don't promise "rolled back / safe to retry" for any NpgsqlException that can escape `CommitAsync` — the outcome is ambiguous. Make "retry-safe" TRUE via idempotency (idempotent UPDATE/archive) + a uniqueness guard on create (retry → conflict, not duplicate), and SAY SO in the error doc. Cross-project: every transactional write whose error code claims rollback. |
| 2026-06-07 | Transactional create/update that COMMITs then RE-QUERIES the entity on a fresh connection has a failure mode: commit succeeds but the post-commit reload throws -> the caller maps it to a "write failed / rolled back" code despite a committed write | PKT-14 Projeler S2 iter1: CreateAsync/UpdateAsync called _repo.GetAsync after commit; a reload NpgsqlException returned INV-OB-071 (rolled back) after a real commit. Fix: build the returned detail INSIDE the same transaction (shared LoadDetailAsync(conn, tx, ...) reading own uncommitted rows) and return it on the result BEFORE commit — no post-commit reload. | **Kural:** return the committed entity from the repo write method by reading it in-tx (LoadDetail(conn, tx) before CommitAsync), not via a separate post-commit query. Eliminates the "committed-but-reported-failed" mislabel. The same in-tx loader serves the standalone GET with a null transaction. Cross-project: any repo that returns the full entity after a transactional write. |

| 2026-06-07 | A typed client that THROWS on a caller-contract / malformed-input guard (ArgumentException, ArgumentOutOfRangeException, InvalidOperationException) will escape the endpoint as an UNHANDLED 500 unless the endpoint maps those throws to coded responses — even when the endpoint pre-validates the "normal" path, because a malformed STORED value (e.g. a control-char secret in tenant settings) still reaches the client guard | PKT-14 Projeler S3 iter0 FAIL (sole blocker, CQ1/CQ5/CQ12/Q3): the endpoint pre-checked empty-secret + instanceId>0, but WapCrmTemplateClient also guards a control-char secret / unattachable header; those throws were uncaught -> 500. Fix: a TYPED FILTERED backstop `catch (Exception ex) when (ex is ArgumentException or InvalidOperationException)` around the client call -> 422 INV-BE-128 (config-invalid) + WARN log; a caller-cancel OperationCanceledException is NOT in the filter so it still propagates. INV-BE-128 doc broadened to not-configured-OR-invalid. | **Kural:** when an endpoint calls a typed client whose input guards THROW (not return a typed outcome), wrap the call in a typed filtered `catch when (ex is ArgumentException or InvalidOperationException)` mapping to a coded response — defensive even if the endpoint pre-validates, because malformed STORED data reaches the guard. Bonus: bu projede 502 ProviderRejected+TransportError ikisini de tasiyor → body'deki providerStatusCode varligi ile ayirt et. Follow-up: send-side WapCrmSendClient (PR-4) ayni canli shape'e karsi PR-4 go-live oncesi dogrulanmali. Cross-project: tum 3rd-party JSON client/DTO eslesmeleri. |

| 2026-06-10 | Lifecycle state-guard'ını akışın TEK kapısına koymak (preview'da reject, confirm'de unutmak) GERÇEK bütünlük deliği — iki-faz akışlarda guard'ı HER giriş noktasına simetrik uygula; idempotent retry'ı korumak için guard'ı "hedef state'e gerçekten geçecek" koşula bağla | PKT-14 SS-D iter0 FAIL Q5 (Codex'in gerçek yakaladığı bug): one-active-run kuralı PreviewSendAsync'e kondu (running/paused → INV-OB-080) ama ConfirmSendAsync sadece paused'ı reddediyordu. Delik: iki kampanyanın preview'i de proje 'draft'ken oluşturulabilir → kampanya A confirm (proje 'running') → kampanya B'nin preview_ready job'ı HÂLÂ confirm edilebilir → İKİNCİ paralel run dispatch. iter1 fix: confirm'de `job.Status=='preview_ready' && proje ∈ {running,paused}` → INV-OB-080 (yalnız FRESH dispatch reject); already-dispatched job'ın re-confirm'i muaf (idempotent canlı status döner). Bonus: Codex ÖNCESİ 4-skeptik adversarial self-verify 2 gerçek bug daha yakalamıştı (SetRunStatusAsync from-state'lerinde 'confirming' eksikti) — HIGH-risk DB/lifecycle işinde review öncesi self-refute pass'i değerli. **Kural:** state-machine guard'ı eklerken o state'e giden TÜM yolları listele (preview+confirm, create+update, vb.) ve her birine uygula; idempotency guard'la çakışıyorsa ayrımı "state'i gerçekten değiştirecek mi" sorusuyla yap. |

Bekleyip pull yapinca SS-D (`c98b03fc`) uzerine temiz kuruldu; commit digerinin wrap docs commit'i (`bde48167`) ustune sorunsuz indi (ayni working tree = onlarin commit'i benim local'ime aninda yansir). KRITIK: benim Outbound+Backend deploy'um SS-D binary'lerini de tasiyacakti → SS-D'nin "DEPLOY PENDING + migration 061 ONCE" notu sayesinde once 061 calistirildi (yoksa Backend `SELECT p.cancelled_count` prod'da kirilirdi). Diger session'in session-memory'ye biraktigi "PARALEL SESSION TESPITI" notu koordinasyonu sagladi. **Kural:** paralel session'da (1) cakisan-dosya isine digerinin commit'i inmeden baslamayin; (2) deploy oncesi `git log origin/master..HEAD` + session-memory'de DEPLOY PENDING taramasi yap — tasinacak bekleyen migration varsa ONCE onu calistir; (3) uncommitted is birakirken session-memory'ye paralel-session notu yaz. Cross-project: ayni repo'da multi-session calisan tum projeler. |
